docker compose exec -T backend bundle exec rails runner '
klass = Api::V1::Sales::CampaignsController

dangerous = %i[
  dispatch
  process
  render
  redirect_to
  request
  response
  params
  session
  cookies
  headers
  action_name
  controller_name
]

dangerous.each do |name|
  owner = begin
    klass.instance_method(name).owner
  rescue NameError
    nil
  end

  puts "#{name}: owner=#{owner}"
end
'
